r"""Coupon Lab | Friday 27 November 2026 | text-only office pilot r2

In the office, paste this entire file into a blank plain-text editor, including
the final "# END OFFICE PAYLOAD" line. Save as COPY_ME_FIRST.py.txt, UTF-8,
in a handoff folder beside the shared pilot app copy. With Miniconda Prompt
open in the handoff folder, run:

    python COPY_ME_FIRST.py.txt --guide

Open FRIDAY_OFFICE_GUIDE.txt. It includes setup, both options for the database,
and the Friday checks. WEEKEND_RETURN.txt is your results sheet. Use only a
copy of the working office app for the pilot. Example installer commands:

    python COPY_ME_FIRST.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" --check
    python COPY_ME_FIRST.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" --apply

The content below is plain-text source. From "# BEGIN OFFICE PAYLOAD" to the
end, the numbered COPY BLOCK lines can be used to paste in smaller sections
in the same order. The script checks the embedded files before extracting or
applying them. Use the working Coupon Lab environment (Python 3.12 or newer)
for the pilot and its tests.
"""

import argparse
import csv
from datetime import datetime
import difflib
import hashlib
import io
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile


PAYLOAD_SHA256 = "3dd86745b05617cd066fca73913cd055aea13394514a5e57726d6180196ff42d"
COPY_ERROR = ("The pasted source is incomplete or changed. Paste the entire text into a "
              "blank file again, from the first line through '# END OFFICE PAYLOAD', then retry.")


def read_payload():
    source = Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").split("\n")
    try:
        start = source.index("# BEGIN OFFICE PAYLOAD")
        end = source.index("# END OFFICE PAYLOAD", start + 1)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR) from None
    if any(line.strip() for line in source[end + 1:]):
        raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR)
    kit = None
    companions = {}
    current = None
    older, newer = [], []

    def finish_record():
        if current is None:
            return
        new_text = "\n".join(newer) + ("\n" if current["new_lf"] else "")
        name = current["name"]
        if current["kind"] == "companion":
            if name in companions:
                raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR)
            companions[name] = new_text
        elif current["kind"] == "app" and name not in kit["files"]:
            old_text = None
            if "old_lf" in current:
                old_text = "\n".join(older) + ("\n" if current["old_lf"] else "")
            kit["files"][name] = {"base": old_text, "new": new_text}
        else:
            raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR)

    for line in source[start + 1:end]:
        if line.startswith("# COPY BLOCK "):
            continue
        if line.startswith("# METADATA ") and kit is None:
            kit = json.loads(line.removeprefix("# METADATA "))
            kit["files"] = {}
        elif line.startswith("# FILE ") and kit is not None:
            finish_record()
            current = json.loads(line.removeprefix("# FILE "))
            older, newer = [], []
        elif current is not None and line[:2] in ("#=", "#-", "#+"):
            if line[1] in "=-":
                older.append(line[2:])
            if line[1] in "=+":
                newer.append(line[2:])
        else:
            raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR)
    finish_record()
    canonical = json.dumps([kit, companions], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    if hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != PAYLOAD_SHA256:
        raise ValueError(COPY_ERROR)
    return kit, companions


def write_guides(companions):
    directory = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    for name, value in companions.items():
        path = directory / name
        try:
            with path.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                handle.write(value)
        except FileExistsError:
            if path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig") != value:
                print(f"Kept your changed {name}. To recover the supplied version, paste this installer into a new folder and run --guide there.")
            else:
                print(f"Already present: {name}")
        else:
            print(f"Created {name}")
    print(f"Open FRIDAY_OFFICE_GUIDE.txt in {directory}")


def body(raw):
    return raw.decode("utf-8-sig").replace("\r\n", "\n")


def encode_like(value, original):
    if b"\r\n" in original:
        value = value.replace("\n", "\r\n")
    codec = "utf-8-sig" if original.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf") else "utf-8"
    return value.encode(codec)


def merge_code(path, current, base, proposed):
    if current == proposed:
        return current
    if current == base:
        return proposed
    if base is None or current is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: file differs or is missing. Use --export to inspect the update.")
    if not shutil.which("git"):
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: office edits detected. Use local Git for the merge, or --export for a manual merge.")
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
        files = [Path(temp) / key for key in ("office", "baseline", "pilot")]
        for dest, content in zip(files, (current, base, proposed)):
            dest.write_bytes(content.encode("utf-8"))
        result = subprocess.run(["git", "merge-file", "-p", *files], capture_output=True)
    if result.returncode:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: overlapping office edits. Use --export, merge the patch in your office copy, and rerun --check.")
    return result.stdout.decode("utf-8")


def merge_products(raw, additions, platform_a):
    reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(body(raw)))
    rows = list(reader)
    fields = reader.fieldnames
    if not fields or any(key not in fields for key in additions[0]):
        raise ValueError("Product mapping columns differ. Review the additions in the payload.")
    extra = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.DictWriter(extra, fields, lineterminator="\r\n" if b"\r\n" in raw else "\n")
    for row in additions:
        matches = [old for old in rows if old["Platform"] in platform_a and old["Source_product"] == row["Source_product"]]
        if len(matches) == 1 and all(matches[0][key].strip().casefold() == row[key].strip().casefold()
                                     for key in ("Common_product", "Barrier", "Capped", "Reviewed")):
            continue
        if matches:
            raise ValueError("A target product mapping is duplicated or has different attributes. Reconcile the two target labels before applying.")
        # Preserve the office's platform spelling when its reviewed reference
        # uses a private brand assignment. Otherwise use the canonical slot.
        spelling = next((old["Platform"] for old in rows if old["Platform"] in platform_a), row["Platform"])
        writer.writerow({**row, "Platform": spelling})
    if not extra.tell():
        return raw
    return raw + (b"" if raw.endswith(b"\n") else b"\n") + extra.getvalue().encode("utf-8")


def checked_target(app, relative):
    path = (app / relative).resolve()
    if not path.is_relative_to(app):
        raise ValueError("A target path lies outside the office copy. Copy its reference inside the pilot folder and update its setting.")
    return path


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Coupon Lab copy-paste office pilot r2")
    parser.add_argument("--app", type=Path, default=Path.cwd())
    modes = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    modes.add_argument("--guide", action="store_true", help="write the guide, office checks and return sheet beside this file")
    modes.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="check the pilot app copy without changing it")
    modes.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true", help="apply to the pilot app copy, with a backup")
    modes.add_argument("--export", type=Path, metavar="NEW_FOLDER", help="write patch references for an office manual merge")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    kit, companions = read_payload()
    if args.guide:
        write_guides(companions)
        return
    if args.export:
        args.export.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        for name, change in kit["files"].items():
            target = checked_target(args.export.resolve(), name)
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_bytes(change["new"].encode("utf-8"))
            if change["base"] is not None:
                patch = difflib.unified_diff(change["base"].splitlines(True), change["new"].splitlines(True), name, name)
                target.with_name(target.name + ".patch").write_text("".join(patch), encoding="utf-8")
        print("Exported pilot files and changes for an internal manual merge.")
        return
    app = args.app.resolve()
    if not (app / "run_coupon_lab.py").is_file() or not (app / "settings.json").is_file():
        raise ValueError("Pass --app pointing to the COPY containing run_coupon_lab.py and settings.json.")
    changes = {}
    for name, change in kit["files"].items():
        path = checked_target(app, name)
        raw = path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None
        current = body(raw) if raw is not None else None
        merged = merge_code(name, current, change["base"], change["new"])
        if merged != current:
            changes[path] = (raw, encode_like(merged, raw if raw is not None else b""))
    settings = checked_target(app, "settings.json")
    raw = settings.read_bytes()
    cfg = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8-sig"))
    daily = cfg.setdefault("daily_reporting", {})
    if not isinstance(daily, dict):
        raise ValueError("Expected daily_reporting to be an object in settings.json.")
    for key, value in kit["settings"].items():
        daily.setdefault(key, value)
    if cfg != json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8-sig")):
        changes[settings] = (raw, encode_like(json.dumps(cfg, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", raw))
    mapping = checked_target(app, cfg.get("references", {}).get("products", "reference/product_mappings.csv"))
    raw = mapping.read_bytes()
    platform_a = {"PLATFORM_A"}
    registry = app / cfg.get("brand_repository", "brand_repository.json")
    if registry.is_file():
        brand = json.loads(registry.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))["brands"]["platform_a"]
        platform_a.update([brand.get("name"), brand.get("source_code"), *brand.get("source_aliases", [])])
    mapped = merge_products(raw, kit["products"], platform_a)
    if mapped != raw:
        changes[mapping] = (raw, mapped)
    print(f"{kit['version']} | {len(changes)} files to update.")
    for path in changes:
        print("  " + str(path.relative_to(app)))
    if not args.apply or not changes:
        return
    backup = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="coupon_daily_backup_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d") + "_", dir=app.parent))
    for path, (raw, _) in changes.items():
        if raw is not None:
            dest = backup / path.relative_to(app)
            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            dest.write_bytes(raw)
    written = []
    try:
        for path, (before, after) in changes.items():
            if (path.read_bytes() if path.exists() else None) != before:
                raise ValueError("Another process changed a target after the check. Close the pilot copy and try again.")
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=path.parent, delete=False) as staged:
                staged.write(after)
            try:
                os.replace(staged.name, path)
            finally:
                Path(staged.name).unlink(missing_ok=True)
            written.append(path)
    except Exception:
        for path in reversed(written):
            before = changes[path][0]
            if before is None:
                path.unlink()
            else:
                path.write_bytes(before)
        raise
    print(f"Installed. Originals are in the sibling folder {backup.name}. Follow FRIDAY_OFFICE_GUIDE.txt.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
        print(f"Pilot installation stopped. {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

#=def _query(state, index=None):
#=    """Resolve compact bulk intentions only on the server, never into stores."""
#=    raw = (state or {}).get('query') or {}
#=    result = {name: list(dict.fromkeys(raw.get(name) or [])) for name in EMPTY_QUERY}
#=    if index is None:
#=        return result
#=    bulk = (state or {}).get('bulk') or {}
#=    scopes = [('reasons', '', bulk.get('reasons'), list(index['reasons']))]
#=    scopes += [('details', parent, choice, list(index.get('detail_catalog', {}).get(parent, {})) or
#=                [token for token, item in index['details'].items() if item['reason'] == parent])
#=               for parent, choice in (bulk.get('details') or {}).items() if parent in index['reasons']]
#=    for suffix, parent, choice, tokens in scopes:
#=        if not isinstance(choice, dict) or choice.get('mode') not in {'include', 'exclude'}:
#=            continue
#=        mode = choice['mode']
#=        omitted = set(choice.get('exceptions') or [])
#=        opposite = set(result[('exclude' if mode == 'include' else 'include') + '_' + suffix])
#=        implicit = [token for token in tokens if token not in omitted and token not in opposite]
#=        result[mode + '_' + suffix] = list(dict.fromkeys(result[mode + '_' + suffix] + implicit))
#=    return result

#=def ytd_metrics(index, selected_keys, baseline_keys, month, covered_months):
#=    """Use January through the selected month, keeping the same RFQ selections.
#=
#=    Only covered months contribute. Missing months are flagged in the label so
#=    partial-year totals cannot appear complete. Recompute the share from summed
#=    CHF; averaging monthly percentages would give small months too much weight.
#=    """
#=    expected=[f'{month[:4]}-{number:02d}' for number in range(1,int(month[-2:])+1)]
#=    available=set(expected).intersection(covered_months)
#=    missing=[period for period in expected if period not in available]
#=    def in_period(keys):
#=        return [key for key in dict.fromkeys(keys)
#=                if index['entries'][key]['row'].get('month') in available]
#=    label='YTD Jan–'+date.fromisoformat(month+'-01').strftime('%b %Y')
#=    if missing:
#=        label+=' · incomplete coverage'
#=    return {'metrics':_metrics(index,in_period(selected_keys),in_period(baseline_keys),
#=                              available=bool(available)),
#=            'label':label,'missing_months':missing}

#=def active_filters(index, query, *, removable=True):
#=    state = _compact(query)
#=    raw = _query(state)
#=    output = []
#=    for group, tokens in raw.items():
#=        mode = 'Include' if group.startswith('include') else 'Exclude'
#=        # A long explicit legacy query is summarized without dropping its values.
#=        shown = tokens if len(tokens) <= 20 else []
#=        if tokens and not shown:
#=            output.append(html.Span(f'{mode}: {len(tokens):,} exact selections', className='mm-filter-chip ' + mode.lower()))
#=        for token in shown:
#=            label = mode + ': ' + _path(index, group, token)
#=            output.append(_filter_chip(label, mode.lower(), group, token, removable))
#=    bulk = state.get('bulk') or {}
#=    choices = [('reasons', '', bulk.get('reasons'))] + [('details', parent, choice)
#=        for parent, choice in (bulk.get('details') or {}).items()]
#=    for kind, parent, choice in choices:
#=        if not choice: continue
#=        mode = choice.get('mode', 'include')
# COPY BLOCK 7
#=        parent_label = index['reasons'].get(parent, {}).get('label', '')
#=        label = mode.title() + ' all ' + ('Reasons' if kind == 'reasons' else parent_label + ' Details')
#=        count = len(choice.get('exceptions') or [])
#=        if count: label += f' · {count:,} exceptions'
#=        output.append(_filter_chip(label, mode, 'bulk-' + kind, parent, removable))
#=    breakdown = _breakdown_selection(state)
#=    if breakdown:
#=        label = _breakdown_label(breakdown)
#=        output.append(_filter_chip(label, 'include', 'breakdown', breakdown['category'],
#=                                   removable, breakdown=True))
#=    return output

#=
#=def _breakdown_label(selection):
#=    return 'Breakdown · ' + DIMENSIONS[selection['dimension']] + ' · ' + selection['category']

#=
#=def selection_title(index, query):
#=    """Compact exact selection intent; bulk actions never expand thousands of labels."""
#=    state = _compact(query)
#=    short, full = [], []
#=    for group, tokens in _query(state).items():
#=        if not tokens: continue
#=        mode = 'Include' if group.startswith('include') else 'Exclude'
#=        kind = 'Reasons' if group.endswith('reasons') else 'scoped Details'
#=        paths = [_path(index, group, token) for token in tokens] if len(tokens) <= 20 else []
#=        exact = mode + ' any ' + kind + ': ' + (' OR '.join(paths) if paths else f'{len(tokens):,} exact selections')
#=        full.append(exact)
#=        short.append(exact if len(exact) <= 135 else mode + ' any of ' + str(len(tokens)) + ' ' + kind)
#=    for chip in active_filters(index, {'bulk': state.get('bulk') or {}}):
#=        label = chip.children[0].children
#=        full.append(label)
#=        short.append(label)
#=    breakdown = _breakdown_selection(state)
#=    if breakdown:
#=        label = _breakdown_label(breakdown)
#=        full.append(label)
#=        short.append(label)
#=    return (' · '.join(short), ' AND '.join(full)) if full else ('All known misses', 'All known misses in the reference population')

#=def bars(records, metric='count', selected=None, empty='No matching RFQs', height=320,
#=         *, omit_assessed_zeros=False, color=None):
#=    """Rank memberships with a common zero and a readable aligned total ledger.
#=
#=    The ledger is presentation only. Stable tokens remain in customdata[0],
#=    including missing-CHF rows; menus retain the full selectable catalog.
#=    """
#=    if omit_assessed_zeros:
#=        records = [item for item in records if (item.get('count') or 0) > 0]
#=    if not records:
#=        return c.empty(empty, height)
#=    metric = metric if metric in {'count', 'chf'} else 'count'
#=    records = sorted(records, key=lambda item: (
#=        item.get(metric) is None, -(item.get(metric) or 0), item['label'].casefold()))
#=    actual_height = max(height, 29 * len(records) + 65)
#=    fig = c._base(actual_height)
#=    values = [_number(item.get(metric)) for item in records]
#=    if metric == 'chf': values = [value / 1_000_000 if value is not None else None for value in values]
#=    customs = [[item['id'], escape(str(item['label'])), item.get('count'),
#=                'CHF ' + c.money(item.get('chf'), compact=False)
#=                if item.get('chf') is not None else 'CHF unavailable'] for item in records]
#=    fig.add_trace(go.Bar(x=values, y=[item['id'] for item in records], orientation='h',
#=        customdata=customs, text=[row[1] for row in customs], textposition='none', width=.56,
#=        marker={'color': ['#298CFF' if item['id'] == selected else (color or c.BLUE) for item in records],
#=                'line': {'color': c.INK, 'width': [1.5 if item['id'] == selected else 0 for item in records]}},
#=        hovertemplate='<b>%{text}</b><br>Distinct missed RFQs: %{customdata[2]:,}'
#=                      '<br>Associated requested amount: %{customdata[3]}<extra></extra>'))
#=    # A right-aligned value column permits exact visual ranking without forcing
#=    # the reader to estimate endpoint positions. It stays fixed when Reason
#=    # segments are split into selected overlap and remaining RFQs.
#=    annotations = []
#=    for item, value, custom in zip(records, values, customs):
#=        label = (c.money(item.get('chf')) if metric == 'chf' else f"{item.get('count', 0):,}")
#=        if value is None: label = '◇ unavailable'
#=        annotations.append(dict(x=1.025, xref='paper', y=item['id'], yref='y',
#=            text=label, xanchor='left', showarrow=False,
#=            font={'size': 10, 'color': '#298CFF' if item['id'] == selected else c.INK},
#=            hovertext=('Associated requested CHF unavailable; distinct RFQ counts remain available.'
#=                       if value is None else custom[3]
#=                       if metric == 'chf' else f"{item['count']:,} distinct missed RFQs")))
#=    # A fixed pixel offset stays compact when the chart has many rows. A
#=    # percentage above a tall plot can reserve hundreds of empty pixels.
#=    annotations.append(dict(x=1.025, xref='paper', y=1, yref='paper', yshift=12, yanchor='bottom',
#=        text='CHF total' if metric == 'chf' else 'RFQs', xanchor='left', showarrow=False,
#=        font={'size': 10, 'color': c.MUTED}))
#=    fig.update_layout(annotations=annotations, margin={'l': 8, 'r': 95, 't': 28, 'b': 35}, showlegend=False)
#=    fig.update_yaxes(tickmode='array', tickvals=[item['id'] for item in records],
#=        ticktext=[('▸ ' if item['id'] == selected else '') + c._label(item['label'], 32)
#=                  for item in records], autorange='reversed', showgrid=False)
#=    fig.update_xaxes(title='Associated requested CHF (m)' if metric == 'chf' else 'Distinct missed RFQs',
#=                     rangemode='tozero', range=c._numeric_range(values),
#=                     tickformat=',~g' if metric == 'chf' else ',.0f',
#=                     dtick=1 if metric == 'count' and max((value or 0 for value in values), default=0) <= 5 else None,
#=                     zeroline=True, zerolinecolor=c.GRID, zerolinewidth=1)
#=    return fig
#=

#=def reason_bars(index, records, overlap_keys, metric='count', *, overlap_label='Included Reason overlap'):
#=    """Keep baseline totals fixed; highlight a deduplicated included-Reason union."""
#=    figure = bars(records, metric, omit_assessed_zeros=True)
#=    if not figure.data: return figure
#=    records = {item['id']: item for item in records}
#=    overlap_keys = set(overlap_keys)
#=    remaining, overlap, custom = [], [], []
#=    for identifier in figure.data[0].y:
#=        record = records[identifier]
#=        shared = sorted(set(record['keys']) & overlap_keys)
#=        shared_count, shared_chf = len(shared), _amount(index, shared)
#=        total = record.get(metric)
#=        shared_value = shared_count if metric == 'count' else shared_chf
#=        if total is None or shared_value is None:
#=            dark, bright = None, None
#=        else:
#=            dark, bright = total - shared_value, shared_value
#=        scale = 1_000_000 if metric == 'chf' else 1
#=        remaining.append(_number(dark) / scale if dark is not None else None)
#=        overlap.append(_number(bright) / scale if bright is not None else None)
#=        custom.append([identifier, escape(str(record['label'])), record['count'],
#=            'CHF ' + c.money(record['chf'], compact=False) if record['chf'] is not None else 'CHF unavailable',
#=            shared_count, 'CHF ' + c.money(shared_chf, compact=False) if shared_chf is not None else 'CHF unavailable',
#=            c.percent(shared_value / total) if total is not None and total > 0 and shared_value is not None else 'Unavailable'])
#=    figure.data[0].x = remaining
#=    figure.data[0].marker.color = c.BLUE
#=    figure.data[0].name = 'Other RFQs'
#=    figure.data[0].customdata = custom
#=    labels = [row[1] for row in custom]
#=    figure.data[0].text = labels
#=    template = ('<b>%{text}</b><br>Total membership: %{customdata[2]:,} RFQs · %{customdata[3]}'
#=                '<br>' + overlap_label + ': %{customdata[4]:,} RFQs · %{customdata[5]}'
#=                '<br>Overlap / this Reason: %{customdata[6]}<extra></extra>')
#=    figure.data[0].hovertemplate = template
#=    figure.add_trace(go.Bar(x=overlap, y=figure.data[0].y, orientation='h', customdata=custom,
#=        name=overlap_label, text=labels, textposition='none', marker_color='#298CFF', width=.56,
#=        marker_line={'color': 'white', 'width': .6}, hovertemplate=template))
#=    figure.update_layout(barmode='stack', showlegend=bool(overlap_keys),
#=                         margin={'l': 8, 'r': 95, 't': 45 if overlap_keys else 28, 'b': 35},
#=                         legend={'traceorder': 'normal', 'y': 1, 'yanchor': 'bottom'})
#=    return figure

#=def _calendar_months(months):
#=    if not months: return []
#=    start, end = sorted(months)[0], sorted(months)[-1]
#=    cursor = date.fromisoformat(start + '-01')
#=    output = []
#=    while cursor.strftime('%Y-%m') <= end:
#=        output.append(cursor.strftime('%Y-%m'))
#=        cursor = date(cursor.year + 1, 1, 1) if cursor.month == 12 else date(cursor.year, cursor.month + 1, 1)
#=    return output

#=def history_figure(index, keys, covered, metric, selected_month, *, active=False):
#=    records = {item['month']: item for item in _engine().history(index, keys)}
#=    months = _calendar_months(covered)
#=    if not months: return c.empty('Report history unavailable', 270)
#=    values, hovers = [], []
#=    missing = []
#=    for month in months:
#=        record = records.get(month, {})
#=        value = record.get(metric, 0) if month in covered else None
#=        values.append(_number(value) / 1_000_000 if metric == 'chf' and value is not None else _number(value))
#=        if month not in covered:
#=            missing.append((month, 'Report coverage unavailable'))
#=            hovers.append('Report coverage unavailable')
#=        else:
#=            amount = record.get('chf', Decimal(0))
#=            count = record.get('count', 0)
#=            hovers.append(f"Distinct missed RFQs: {count:,}<br>Associated requested amount: " +
#=                          ('CHF ' + c.money(amount, compact=False) if amount is not None else 'CHF unavailable'))
#=            if value is None: missing.append((month, 'CHF unavailable; RFQ count remains available'))
#=    fig = c._base(270)
#=    color = '#298CFF' if active else c.BLUE
#=    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(x=months, y=values, mode='lines+markers', connectgaps=False,
#=        line={'color': color, 'width': 1.8}, marker={
#=            'size': [9 if month == selected_month else 5 for month in months],
#=            'symbol': ['diamond' if month == selected_month else 'circle' for month in months],
#=            'line': {'color': 'white', 'width': 1},
#=            'color': [c.GOLD if month == selected_month else color for month in months]},
#=        customdata=months, text=hovers,
#=        hovertemplate='<b>%{x}</b><br>%{text}<extra></extra>'))
#=    # These symbols stand above the plot to mark observations with no available value.
#=    annotations = [dict(x=month, xref='x', y=1.025, yref='paper', text='◇',
#=        hovertext=message, showarrow=False, font={'size': 12, 'color': c.MUTED})
#=        for month, message in missing]
#=    if missing:
#=        annotations.append(dict(x=0, xref='paper', y=1.12, yref='paper',
#=            text='◇ unavailable · gaps are retained', xanchor='left', showarrow=False,
#=            font={'size': 10, 'color': c.MUTED}))
#=    fig.update_layout(annotations=annotations, showlegend=False,
#=                      margin={'l': 8, 'r': 15, 't': 30 if missing else 12, 'b': 35})
#=    fig.update_xaxes(type='category', title=None, tickmode='array', tickvals=months,
#=        ticktext=[date.fromisoformat(month + '-01').strftime('%b<br>%Y') for month in months],
#=        showgrid=False)
#=    fig.update_yaxes(title='Associated requested CHF (m)' if metric == 'chf' else 'Distinct missed RFQs',
#=                     title_font={'size': 10}, rangemode='tozero', range=c._numeric_range(values),
#=                     dtick=1 if metric == 'count' and max((value or 0 for value in values), default=0) <= 5 else None,
#=                     tickformat=',~g' if metric == 'chf' else ',.0f')
#=    return fig

#=def timeline_figure(series, metric, selected_day=None, *, active=False):
#=    """Present aggregate daily/hourly amounts without exposing RFQ records.

#=def _case_detail(index, key):
#=    """Render only whitelisted evidence; raw source values remain plain text."""
#=    if not key or key not in index['entries']:
#=        return html.P('No matching RFQ to inspect.', className='mm-muted')
#=    entry, row = index['entries'][key], index['entries'][key]['row']
#=    reason_details = []

#=        for detail in assessment.get('details', {}).values():

#=                *[html.Pre(str(message), className='mm-source-message')
#=                  for message in dict.fromkeys(detail.get('raw_messages') or [])]], className='mm-evidence-detail'))
#=        configuration_gap = assessment.get('configuration_assessment_incomplete') is True
#=        heading = [index['reasons'][identifier]['label']]
#=        if configuration_gap:
#=            heading.append(html.Span(' · configuration assessment incomplete', className='mm-muted'))
#=        gap_note = [html.P('Configuration could not be fully assessed. This recorded lifetime failure remains included; '
#=                          'a configuration-based exclusion has not been established.', className='mm-note')]
#=        reason_details.append(html.Details([html.Summary(heading),
#=            *(gap_note if configuration_gap else []),
#=            *([html.P(assessment['detail_availability_note'], className='mm-note')] if assessment.get('detail_availability_note') else []),
#=            *(messages or [html.P('No linked Details recorded.', className='mm-muted')])],
#=            className='mm-evidence-reason'))
#=    supports = []
#=    for item in row.get('admin_support') or []:
#=        def flag_label(name):
#=            if name in item.get('invalid_provider_flags', []): return 'unknown (invalid flag)'
#=            if name in item.get('missing_provider_flags', []): return 'disabled (missing configuration)'
#=            return 'enabled' if item.get(name) == 1 else 'disabled' if item.get(name) == 0 else 'unknown'
#=        flags = ', '.join(name + ': ' + flag_label(name) for name in ('ISSUER', 'PROVIDER_A', 'PROVIDER_B', 'PROVIDER_C'))
#=        supports.append([item.get('symbol'), item.get('source_identifier'), item.get('status'), flags])
#=    providers = [[item.get('provider'), item.get('part_type'),
#=                  'Yes' if item.get('asked') is True else 'Not established',
#=                  'Yes' if item.get('received') is True else 'Not established',
#=                  item.get('quote_type'), item.get('timing')]
#=                 for item in row.get('providers') or [] if item.get('part_type') in {'TRS', 'PUT', 'DIP'}]
#=    observations = []
#=    for observation in row.get('observations') or []:
#=        # Keep omitted/suppressed reporting codes inspectable without copying
#=        # source rows, numerical quote levels or private connection metadata.
#=        metadata = [('Provider', observation.get('provider')),
#=                    ('Hedge detail', observation.get('detail_id')),
#=                    ('Record', observation.get('record_id')),
#=                    ('Version', observation.get('version'))]
#=        notes = [label + ': ' + str(value) for label, value in metadata if value not in (None, '')]
#=        if observation.get('event_timestamp'):
#=            notes.append('Recorded: ' + swiss_timestamp(observation['event_timestamp']))
#=        message = observation.get('message')
#=        observations.append(html.Div([
#=            html.Strong(str(observation.get('source') or 'Source unavailable') + ' · ' +
#=                        str(observation.get('code') or 'Code unavailable')),

#=            html.Pre(str(message) if message not in (None, '') else 'Message unavailable',
#=                     className='mm-source-message'),
#=        ], className='mm-evidence-detail'))
#=    report_time = (swiss_timestamp(row.get('feedback_created_on') or row.get('timestamp'))
#=                   if row.get('feedback_time_available') is not False else 'time unavailable')
#=    return html.Div([
#=        html.Div([html.Strong('RFQ ' + str(row['rfq_id'])), html.Span(product_label(row)),
#=                  html.Span(formatted(row.get('notional_chf'), 'money'))], className='mm-selected-rfq'),

#=        *reason_details,

#=def _scope_data(index, snapshot, platform, month, population, filters, analysis, state):
#=    """Cache immutable data selections, never mutable figures or browser state."""
#=    engine = _engine()
#=    runtime = index.get('runtime')
#=    def cached(kind, token, factory):
#=        return runtime.cached(kind, token, factory) if runtime is not None else factory()
#=    def cohort_keys(choices):
#=        if runtime is not None:
#=            return list(runtime.cohort_keys(platform, population, choices, snapshot.get('ticket_size_bands')))
#=        return [engine.key(row) for row in p.cohort(snapshot['rows'], platform, population, choices,
#=                                                   snapshot.get('ticket_size_bands'))]
#=    def digest(value):
#=        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, default=str).encode()).hexdigest()
#=    population_token = ('missed-population', platform, month, population, digest(filters))
#=    def population_data():
#=        keys = cohort_keys(filters)
#=        covered = dm.covered_months(snapshot, platform)
#=        previous_month = _month_before(month)
#=        def summary(period):
#=            if period not in covered:
#=                return m.unavailable_summary()
#=            return m.monthly([index['entries'][key]['row'] for key in keys
#=                              if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == period])
#=        current = summary(month)
#=        previous = summary(previous_month)
#=        expected = [f'{month[:4]}-{n:02d}' for n in range(1, int(month[-2:]) + 1)]
#=        available = set(expected).intersection(covered)
#=        ytd = m.monthly([index['entries'][key]['row'] for key in keys
#=                        if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] in available]) if available else m.unavailable_summary()
#=        baseline = [key for key in keys if index['entries'][key]['row'].get('qualifying_quote') is False]
#=        return {'keys': keys, 'baseline': baseline, 'covered': covered, 'current': current,
#=                'previous': previous, 'ytd': ytd, 'previous_month': previous_month}
#=    parent = cached('population', population_token, population_data)
#=    query = _query(state, index)
#=    breakdown_selection = _breakdown_selection(state)
#=    focus = (state.get('reason'), state.get('detail'))
#=    selection_token = ('missed-selection', population_token, digest(analysis),
#=                       digest(query), digest((breakdown_selection, focus) if breakdown_selection else None))
#=
#=    def selection_data():
#=        candidates = set(cohort_keys(analysis)) if any(analysis.values()) else None
#=        baseline = [key for key in parent['baseline'] if candidates is None or key in candidates]
#=        current_base = [key for key in baseline if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == month]
#=        result = engine.select(index, baseline, query)
#=        matched = result['matched']
#=        breakdown_base = [key for key in matched if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == month]
#=        selected = breakdown_base
#-        reason_query = {**query, 'include_details': [], 'exclude_details': []}
#+        reason_query = {**query, 'include_details': []}
#=        reason_context = [key for key in engine.select(index, baseline, reason_query)['matched']
#=                          if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == month]
#=        population_current = [key for key in parent['baseline'] if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == month]
#=        population_previous = [key for key in parent['baseline'] if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == parent['previous_month']]
#=        if breakdown_selection:
#=            # Match the bar's category on each period's RFQs using the same
#=            # attribution as the breakdown chart, including linked provider codes.
#=            member_keys = set(engine.breakdown_members(index, [*matched, *reason_context],
#=                breakdown_selection['dimension'], breakdown_selection['category'],
#=                reason=focus[0], detail=focus[1], spec=query, bands=snapshot.get('ticket_size_bands')))
#=            matched = [key for key in matched if key in member_keys]
#=            selected = [key for key in breakdown_base if key in member_keys]
#=            reason_context = [key for key in reason_context if key in member_keys]
#+        # Removing a reason removes each matching RFQ (including its CHF)
#+        # from every other reason bar. Includes still highlight overlaps.
#+        chart_keys = current_base
#+        exclusions = {name: query[name] for name in ('exclude_reasons', 'exclude_details') if query[name]}
#+        if exclusions:
#+            chart_keys = engine.select(index, current_base, exclusions)['matched']
#=        previous_keys = [key for key in matched if index['entries'][key]['row']['month'] == parent['previous_month']]
#=        return {'parent': parent, 'baseline': baseline, 'current_base': current_base,
#=                'selected': selected, 'breakdown_base': breakdown_base,
#=                'matched': matched, 'query': query, 'reason_context': reason_context,
#=                'now': _metrics(index, selected, population_current, available=month in parent['covered']),
#=                'previous': _metrics(index, previous_keys, population_previous)
#=                            if parent['previous_month'] in parent['covered'] else None,
#=                'ytd': ytd_metrics(index, matched, parent['baseline'], month, parent['covered']),
#-                'reason_records': engine.aggregate_reasons(index, current_base), 'available': month in parent['covered']}
#+                'reason_records': engine.aggregate_reasons(index, chart_keys), 'available': month in parent['covered']}
#=    return cached('selection', selection_token, selection_data)

#=def _detail_results(index, data, state, metric):
#=    reason = state.get('reason')
#=    records = _engine().aggregate_details(index, data['reason_context'], reason=reason,
#=                                         include_keys=False) if reason in index['reasons'] else []
#=    brand_registry = registry_for(index.get('snapshot'))
#=    search = str(state.get('detail_search') or '').strip().casefold()
#=    records = [item for item in records if (item.get('count') or 0) > 0 and
#=               (not search or search in display_text(item['label'], brand_registry).casefold())]
#=    records.sort(key=lambda item: (item.get(metric) is None, -(item.get(metric) or 0),
#=                                  item['label'].casefold(), item['id']))
#=    page = state.get('detail_page', 0)
#=    if state.get('_reveal_detail') and state.get('detail'):
#=        position = next((n for n, item in enumerate(records) if item['id'] == state['detail']), None)
#=        if position is not None: page = position // DETAIL_PAGE_SIZE
#=    visible, page, pages, count = _page(records, page, DETAIL_PAGE_SIZE)
#=    figure = bars(visible, metric, selected=state.get('detail'),
#=                  empty='No recorded Details match this view' if reason else 'Select a Reason to inspect its Details')
#=    return {'mm-details.figure': figure,
#=            'mm-detail-title.children': 'Details · ' + index['reasons'][reason]['label'] if reason in index['reasons'] else 'Details',
#=            'mm-detail-page-label.children': f'{page * DETAIL_PAGE_SIZE + 1 if count else 0}–{min((page + 1) * DETAIL_PAGE_SIZE, count)} of {count:,} Details',
#=            'mm-detail-previous.disabled': page == 0, 'mm-detail-next.disabled': page + 1 >= pages}

#=def _case_results(index, data, state, search='', *, evidence_only=False):
#=    if not data.get('available', True):
#=        return {'mm-case-table.data': [], 'mm-case-table.page_count': 1,
#=                'mm-case-count.children': 'RFQ list unavailable for this report period',
#=                'mm-case-detail.children': html.P('Report unavailable for the selected platform and month.', className='mm-muted')}
#=    if not state.get('cases_open'):
#=        return {'mm-case-table.data': [], 'mm-case-table.page_count': 1,
#=                'mm-case-count.children': f"{len(data['selected']):,} matching RFQs · expand to inspect",
#=                'mm-case-detail.children': []}
#=    records = _case_records(index, data['selected'], search)
#=    sort = state.get('case_sort') or []
#=    allowed = {name for name, _, _ in CASE_SPECS}
#=    def compare(left, right):
#=        for order in sort:
#=            field = order.get('column_id')
#=            if field not in allowed: continue
#=            if field == 'amount':
#=                a = index['entries'][left['id']]['row'].get('notional_chf')
#=                b = index['entries'][right['id']]['row'].get('notional_chf')
#=            else:
#=                a, b = str(left.get(field) or '').casefold(), str(right.get(field) or '').casefold()
#=            if a is None or b is None:
#=                result = 0 if a is None and b is None else 1 if a is None else -1
#=            else:
#=                result = (a > b) - (a < b)
#=                if order.get('direction') == 'desc': result = -result
#=            if result: return result
#=        return (left['id'] > right['id']) - (left['id'] < right['id'])
#=    if sort: records.sort(key=cmp_to_key(compare))
#=    visible, page, pages, count = _page(records, state.get('case_page'), CASE_PAGE_SIZE)
#=    available = {item['id'] for item in records}
#=    selected_case = state.get('rfq_key')
#=    if selected_case not in available:
#=        selected_case = visible[0]['id'] if visible else None
#=    detail = _case_detail(index, selected_case)
#=    if evidence_only: return {'mm-case-detail.children': detail}
#=    return {'mm-case-table.data': visible, 'mm-case-table.page_count': pages,
#=            'mm-case-count.children': f'{page * CASE_PAGE_SIZE + 1 if count else 0}–{min((page + 1) * CASE_PAGE_SIZE, count)} of {count:,} matching RFQs',
#=            'mm-case-detail.children': detail}

#=    selection_active = base_selection_active or bool(breakdown_selection)
#=    data = _scope_data(index, snapshot, platform, month, population, filters, analysis, state)

#=        return _detail_results(index, data, state, metric)

#=    reason = state.get('reason') if state.get('reason') in index['reasons'] else None
#=    detail = state.get('detail') if state.get('detail') in index['details'] else None
#=    # Total bar lengths are the attribute-filtered baseline. Highlights are the
#=    # effective selected RFQ intersection, so exclusions remain whole-RFQ rules.
#=    overlap = selected if (breakdown_selection or detail or query['include_reasons'] or query['include_details']) else []
#=    overlap_label = ('Selected breakdown overlap' if breakdown_selection else
#=                     'Selected Detail overlap' if detail else 'Included Reason overlap')
#=    reason_figure = reason_bars(index, data['reason_records'], overlap, metric, overlap_label=overlap_label)
#=    breakdown = engine.breakdown(index, data['breakdown_base'], dimension, reason=reason, detail=detail,
#=                                  spec=query, bands=snapshot.get('ticket_size_bands'))
#=    attributed = dimension == 'underlying' and (reason == 'configuration' or
#=        'configuration' in query['include_reasons'] or any(index['details'].get(token, {}).get('reason') == 'configuration'
#=                                                        for token in query['include_details']))
#=    breakdown_note = ('Affected constituents only; basket-wide constraints are shown separately.' if attributed else
#=                      'Basket exposure. Select a configuration Reason for affected-constituent counts.') if dimension == 'underlying' else ''
#=    if dimension == 'underlying':
#=        breakdown_note += ' Constituent exposures overlap; do not add their CHF amounts.'
#=    if breakdown:

#=        breakdown_note = (breakdown_note + ' ' + click_hint).strip()
#=    selection_label, full_selection_label = selection_title(index, state)
#=    ytd = data['ytd']

#=        return {'overall_missed_chf': summary.get('missed_chf'), 'overall_missed_rfqs': summary.get('missed_rfq_count'),
#=                'overall_missed_rate': summary.get('missed_rate')}

#=def transition(state, event, value, index):
#=    """Atomic UI intentions; bulk selection stays compact across all pages."""
#=    original = deepcopy(state or {})
#=    state = deepcopy(original)
#=    raw = _query(state)
#=    effective = _query(state, index)
#=    bulk = deepcopy(state.get('bulk') or {})
#=    bulk.setdefault('details', {})
#=    if event == 'clear': return {}
#=    if event == 'breakdown' and not _breakdown_selection({'breakdown': value}):
#=        return original
#=    if event == 'detail' and value in index['details']:
#=        if index['details'][value]['reason'] in effective['exclude_reasons']: return original
#=    if event in {'filter', 'bulk'} and value.get('kind') == 'detail':
#=        if value.get('reason') in effective['exclude_reasons']: return original
#=    state.pop('rfq_key', None)
#=    state.pop('timeline_day', None)
#=    state.pop('timeline_scope', None)
#=    state['detail_page'] = 0
#=    state['_event'] = 'selection'
#=    state['_reveal_detail'] = False
#=
#=    def choice(kind, parent):
#=        return bulk.get('reasons') if kind == 'reason' else bulk['details'].get(parent)
#=    def omit_default(kind, parent, token):
#=        item = choice(kind, parent)
#=        if item:
#=            item['exceptions'] = list(dict.fromkeys((item.get('exceptions') or []) + [token]))
#=    def clear_include_bulk(suffix):
#=        if suffix == 'reasons' and bulk.get('reasons', {}).get('mode') == 'include': bulk.pop('reasons', None)
#=        if suffix == 'details':
#=            bulk['details'] = {parent: item for parent, item in bulk['details'].items() if item.get('mode') != 'include'}
#=    if event == 'breakdown':
#=        if _breakdown_selection(state) == value:
#=            state.pop('breakdown')
#=        else:
#=            state['breakdown'] = value
#=    elif event == 'bulk':
#=        kind, mode, parent = value.get('kind'), value.get('mode'), value.get('reason', '')
#=        if kind not in {'reason', 'detail'} or mode not in {'include', 'exclude'}: return original
#=        if kind == 'reason':
#=            bulk['reasons'] = {'mode': mode, 'exceptions': []}
#=            raw['include_reasons'], raw['exclude_reasons'] = [], []
#=            if mode == 'exclude':
#=                raw['include_details'], raw['exclude_details'] = [], []
#=                bulk['details'] = {}
#=                state.pop('reason', None)
#=                state.pop('detail', None)
#=        elif parent in index['reasons']:
#=            for group in ('include_details', 'exclude_details'):
#=                raw[group] = [token for token in raw[group] if index['details'].get(token, {}).get('reason') != parent]
#=            bulk['details'][parent] = {'mode': mode, 'exceptions': []}
#=            state['reason'] = parent
#=            state.pop('detail', None)
#=    elif event == 'reason':
#=        if value not in index['reasons']: return original
#=        if state.get('reason') == value and effective['include_reasons'] == [value]:
#=            raw['include_reasons'] = []
#=            state.pop('reason', None)
#=        else:
#=            raw['include_reasons'] = [value]
#=            raw['exclude_reasons'] = [token for token in raw['exclude_reasons'] if token != value]
#=            state['reason'] = value
#=            state.update(menu_reason=value, menu_page=0, menu_search='')
#=        clear_include_bulk('reasons')
#=        if bulk.get('reasons', {}).get('mode') == 'exclude': omit_default('reason', '', value)
#=        raw['include_details'] = []
#=        clear_include_bulk('details')
#=        state.pop('detail', None)
#=        state['detail_search'] = ''
#=    elif event == 'detail':
#=        if value not in index['details']: return original
#=        parent = index['details'][value]['reason']
#=        if state.get('detail') == value and effective['include_details'] == [value]:
#=            raw['include_details'] = []
#=            state.pop('detail', None)
#=        else:
#=            raw['include_details'] = [value]
#=            raw['exclude_details'] = [token for token in raw['exclude_details'] if token != value]
#=            state.update(detail=value, reason=parent, detail_search='', _reveal_detail=True)
#=        clear_include_bulk('details')
#=        if choice('detail', parent) and choice('detail', parent).get('mode') == 'exclude': omit_default('detail', parent, value)
#=    elif event == 'filter':
#=        kind, mode = value.get('kind'), value.get('mode')
#=        if kind not in {'reason', 'detail'} or mode not in {'include', 'exclude'}: return original
#=        token = value.get('reason') if kind == 'reason' else value.get('detail')
#=        catalog = index['reasons'] if kind == 'reason' else index['details']
#=        if token not in catalog: return original
#=        parent = value.get('reason', '') if kind == 'detail' else ''
#=        if kind == 'detail' and catalog[token]['reason'] != parent: return original
#=        suffix = 'reasons' if kind == 'reason' else 'details'
#=        group, other = mode + '_' + suffix, ('exclude' if mode == 'include' else 'include') + '_' + suffix
#=        item = choice(kind, parent)
#=        selected = token in effective[group]
#=        if selected:
#=            raw[group] = [existing for existing in raw[group] if existing != token]
#=            if item and item.get('mode') == mode: omit_default(kind, parent, token)
#=        else:
#=            raw[other] = [existing for existing in raw[other] if existing != token]
#=            if item and item.get('mode') == mode:
#=                item['exceptions'] = [existing for existing in item.get('exceptions', []) if existing != token]
#=            else:
#=                raw[group] = list(dict.fromkeys(raw[group] + [token]))
#=                if item: omit_default(kind, parent, token)
#=        if kind == 'reason' and mode == 'exclude' and not selected:
#=            for child_group in ('include_details', 'exclude_details'):
#=                raw[child_group] = [child for child in raw[child_group] if index['details'].get(child, {}).get('reason') != token]
#=            bulk['details'].pop(token, None)
#=            if index['details'].get(state.get('detail'), {}).get('reason') == token: state.pop('detail', None)
#=        if mode == 'include' and not selected:
#=            if kind == 'reason': state['reason'] = token; state.update(menu_reason=token, menu_page=0, menu_search=''); state.pop('detail', None)
#=            else: state.update(detail=token, menu_reason=parent, detail_search='', _reveal_detail=True)
#=        elif kind == 'detail' and state.get('detail') == token: state.pop('detail', None)
#=        elif kind == 'reason' and state.get('reason') == token: state.pop('reason', None); state.pop('detail', None)
#=    elif event == 'remove':
#=        group, token = value.get('group'), value.get('token')
#=        if group == 'breakdown':
#=            selection = _breakdown_selection(state)
#=            if selection and token == selection['category']:
#=                state.pop('breakdown')
#=        elif group == 'bulk-reasons': bulk.pop('reasons', None)
#=        elif group == 'bulk-details': bulk['details'].pop(token, None)
#=        elif group in raw:
#=            raw[group] = [existing for existing in raw[group] if existing != token]
#=            if group == 'include_details' and state.get('detail') == token: state.pop('detail', None)
#=            if group == 'include_reasons' and state.get('reason') == token: state.pop('reason', None); state.pop('detail', None)
#=    state['query'] = raw
#=    if bulk.get('reasons') or bulk.get('details'): state['bulk'] = bulk
#=    else: state.pop('bulk', None)
#=    return state

#=def register_callbacks(app, snapshot, settings, index):
#=    """One local reducer; light navigation triggers only its affected outputs."""
#=    globals_ = [Input('platform', 'value'), Input('month', 'value'), Input('population', 'value')]
#=    globals_ += [Input('filter-' + name, 'value') for name in BUSINESS_FILTERS]
#=    globals_.append(Input('filter-received', 'value'))
#=    inputs = globals_ + [Input('analysis-missed-state', 'data'), Input('analysis-missed-clear', 'n_clicks'),
#=        Input('reset', 'n_clicks'), Input('mm-reasons', 'clickData'), Input('mm-details', 'clickData'),
#=        Input('mm-timeline', 'clickData'), Input('mm-timeline-back', 'n_clicks'),
#=        Input('mm-breakdown', 'clickData'), Input('mm-dimension', 'value'),

#=def properties(node, result=None):
#=    result = {} if result is None else result
#=    if isinstance(node, dict):
#=        props = node.get('props', {})
#=        identifier = props.get('id')
#=        if isinstance(identifier, str):
#=            if identifier in result: raise AssertionError('Repeated layout identifier: ' + identifier)

#=            result[identifier] = props
#=        for value in node.values():
#=            if isinstance(value, (list, dict)): properties(value, result)
#=    elif isinstance(node, list):
#=        for item in node: properties(item, result)
#=    return result

#=    if isinstance(node, dict):
#=        identifier = node.get('props', {}).get('id')
#=        if isinstance(identifier, dict): result.append(identifier)
#=        for value in node.values():
#=            if isinstance(value, (list, dict)): pattern_ids(value, result)
#=    elif isinstance(node, list):

#=            for child in item: visit(child)

#=    def invoke(self, trigger, values=None, state=None, pattern=None):
#=        callback_key, metadata = next(iter(self.app.callback_map.items()))
#=        defaults = {('platform', 'value'): 'PLATFORM_A', ('month', 'value'): '2026-09',
#=                    ('population', 'value'): 'all', ('missed-state', 'data'): state or {},
#=                    ('mm-case-table', 'data'): []}

#=        for item in metadata['inputs']:
#=            if item['id'].startswith('{') and pattern is not None and pattern['type'] in item['id']:
#=                inputs.append([{'id': pattern, 'property': item['property'], 'value': 1}])
#=            else:
#=                inputs.append({**item, 'value': defaults.get((item['id'], item['property']),
#=                              [] if item['id'].startswith('{') else None)})
#=        states = [{**item, 'value': defaults.get((item['id'], item['property']))} for item in metadata['state']]
#=        response = self.app.server.test_client().post('/_dash-update-component', json={
#=            'output': callback_key,
#=            'outputs': [{'id': item.component_id, 'property': item.component_property} for item in metadata['output']],
#=            'inputs': inputs, 'state': states, 'changedPropIds': trigger if isinstance(trigger, list) else [trigger]})
#=        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.get_data(as_text=True))
#=        return response.get_json()['response']

# FILE {"kind": "app", "name": "coupon_lab/daily.py", "new_lf": true}
#+"""Daily Reporting for the local desktop app and the daily-capture command.
#+
#+Read SLD only for Today or an explicit capture. The SQLite store is opened on
#+first use so a shared-folder problem cannot prevent opening the monthly report.
#+All dates shown to the desk follow the Zurich calendar.
#+"""
#+from __future__ import annotations
#+
#+from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
#+from pathlib import Path
#+from threading import Lock
#+from uuid import uuid4
#+from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
#+
#+from .daily_source import demo_day, read_day
#+from .daily_store import DailyStore
#+
#+
#+class DailyError(ValueError):
#+    safe_message = True
#+
#+
#+def _date(value):
#+    try:
#+        result = date.fromisoformat(value)
#+        if value == result.isoformat():
#+            return result
#+    except (ValueError, TypeError):
#+        pass
#+    raise DailyError("Daily Reporting needs a date in YYYY-MM-DD format.")
#+
#+
#+class DailyService:
#+    def __init__(self, cfg, demo=False):
#+        self.cfg = cfg
#+        self.demo = demo
#+        self.options = cfg.get("daily_reporting", {})
#+        self.lock = Lock()
#+        self._store = None
#+        self._last_live = None
#+
#+    @property
#+    def store(self):
#+        with self.lock:
#+            if self._store is None:
#+                directory = Path(self.cfg["_root"])
#+                filename = ("../coupon_lab_reporting/daily_demo.sqlite3" if self.demo else
#+                            self.options.get("store", "../coupon_lab_reporting/daily.sqlite3"))
#+                if not isinstance(filename, str) or not filename.strip():
#+                    raise DailyError("Set daily_reporting.store to the shared reporting SQLite file.")
#+                self._store = DailyStore((directory / filename).resolve())
#+            return self._store
#+
#+    def today(self):
#+        return datetime.now(ZoneInfo("Europe/Zurich")).date()
#+
#+    def previous_day(self):
#+        holidays = self.options.get("holidays", [])
#+        if not isinstance(holidays, list):
#+            raise DailyError("Set daily_reporting.holidays to a list of Zurich dates in YYYY-MM-DD format.")
#+        closed = {_date(item) for item in holidays}
#+        day = self.today() - timedelta(days=1)
#+        while day.weekday() >= 5 or day in closed:
#+            day -= timedelta(days=1)
#+        return day
#+
#+    def days(self):
#+        return sorted({self.today().isoformat(), self.previous_day().isoformat(), *self.store.days()}, reverse=True)
#+
#+    def _day(self, value):
#+        day = _date(value)
#+        if day > self.today():
#+            raise DailyError("Choose today or a past Zurich reporting day.")
#+        return day
#+
#+    def _read(self, day):
#+        return demo_day(day) if self.demo else read_day(self.cfg, day)
#+
#+    def load(self, day):
#+        selected = self._day(day)
#+        if selected != self.today():
#+            return self.store.load(day)
#+        # Only fresh reads replace the last-good source. Always combine it with
#+        # the latest shared manual revisions, including after a source failure.
#+        store = self.store
#+        with self.lock:
#+            try:
#+                source = self._read(selected)
#+            except Exception as exc:
#+                source = self._last_live
#+                if source and source.get("day") != day:
#+                    source = None
#+                from .errors import PipelineError
#+                detail = str(exc) if isinstance(exc, PipelineError) or getattr(exc, "safe_message", False) else "Check the office reader and configured daily source."
#+                failure = "SLD refresh failed. " + detail
#+            else:
#+                self._last_live = source
#+                failure = None
#+        view = store.load(day, source=source)
#+        if failure:
#+            view["warnings"] = [failure, *view.get("warnings", [])]
#+            view["refresh_error"] = failure
#+        return view
#+
#+    def capture(self, day):
#+        selected = self._day(day)
#+        store = self.store
#+        if store.snapshot(day) is None:

# FILE {"kind": "app", "name": "coupon_lab/daily_source.py", "new_lf": true}
#+"""Read and calculate one Daily Reporting day from read-only SLD.
#+
#+Inputs are the existing full settings dict and a report date. Paths resolve
#+against settings['_root']. The existing sld.connection_module supplies the
#+dedicated connection. No bank driver is selected or imported here.
#+
#+Requests use request creation in [Zurich midnight, next Zurich midnight).
#+Trades use product INITIAL_FIXING in that window and ACTIVE/ISSUING state.
#+One request ID is one RFQ. Child offers never multiply its notional.
#+
#+Office choices live in daily_reporting.source. Optional request_sql/trade_sql
#+or request_sql_file/trade_sql_file replace the corresponding SELECT and receive day_start/day_end DB-API binds
#+(naive Zurich datetimes). flow_profile_expression defaults to the observed
#+p.USER_PROFILE_NAME candidate. Set profile_confirmed=true after comparing it
#+with the Excel Manual profile. Before that, category figures are provisional.
#+Missing/unrecognized profile markers and conflicting observations stay unknown.
#+underlyings_expression defaults to the observed r.UNDERLYINGS text field.
#+
#+Request SELECT aliases: RFQ_ID, REQUEST_CREATED_AT, FLOW_PROFILE.
#+Trade SELECT aliases: RFQ_ID, PRODUCT_ID, PRODUCT_STATE, INITIAL_FIXING,
#+FLOW_PROFILE, PRODUCT_TYPE, AMOUNT, CURRENCY, UNDERLYINGS, PLATFORM.
#+Optional trade aliases: PROVIDER (office-confirmed executed provider),
#+PNL_NATIVE (office-confirmed native-currency issuance PnL), or MARGIN_PA and
#+LIFETIME_YEARS. The latter calculation requires explicit positive margin_scale
#+in daily_reporting.source, e.g. '0.01' for a confirmed percentage representation.
#+The office SELECT supplies lifetime in years under its agreed convention.
#+Default SQL supplies NULL PnL, margin and lifetime, with no financial guesses.
#+
#+Default provider evidence is distinct COUNTERPARTY on selected TRS/DIP/PUT
#+hedge rows. It remains labelled provisional. Conflicting counterparties remain
#+unknown. An office PROVIDER alias is usable only with provider_confirmed=true.
#+
#+FX uses each currency's latest available reviewed monthly CHF_per_unit factor
#+on or before the reporting month. Each used month/factor is retained. Empty
# COPY BLOCK 15
#+rate cells remain unavailable. Missing amount/rate/PnL preserves the trade row.
#+An unusable FX reference preserves CHF identity and leaves foreign conversion
#+unavailable. Failed optional hedge enrichment preserves both cohort reads and
#+their amounts, with unavailable provider evidence. A failed request or trade
#+cohort read is fatal and must leave the caller's last-good source unchanged.
#+Outputs contain JSON-safe strings for Decimal values, integer request counts,
#+explicit coverage/warnings, and one row per eligible traded RFQ.
#+"""
#+from __future__ import annotations
#+

#+from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone
#+from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation, localcontext

#+import re
#+from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

#+                 "FLOW_PROFILE", "PRODUCT_TYPE", "AMOUNT", "CURRENCY",

#+PROVIDER_COLUMNS = ("PRODUCT_ID", "COUNTERPARTY", "PART_TYPE", "IS_WINNER")

#+"""Shared Daily Reporting state and selected-day report composition.
#+
#+Use a separate, configured SQLite file for application-owned state. The source
#+reader remains outside this module and outside write transactions. capture()
#+stores one immutable SLD result per day. load() combines that saved result (or
#+an explicitly supplied live result) with the latest committed manual entries.
#+
#+Writes use a short BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction, expected revisions and durable
#+retry outcomes. SQLite rollback journaling is required. No WAL is enabled.
#+Filesystem locking, two-PC access and backup/restore still need office testing.
#+All money is decimal text. No office connection or database driver is loaded.
#+"""

#+def _entry(view, selected):
#+    try:
#+        identifier, revision = json.loads(selected)
#+        if not isinstance(revision, int) or isinstance(revision, bool) or revision < 1:
#+            raise ValueError
#+    except (TypeError, ValueError):
#+        raise DailyUIError('Select a current manual entry before linking or removing it.') from None

#+        raise DailyUIError('Select a current manual entry. Refresh if a colleague changed it.')
#+    # Polling refreshes the list, but never upgrades the revision the operator
#+    # selected. The store must reject an action on an older selected revision.
#+    return {**result, 'revision': revision}

#+        result.append({'label': label, 'value': json.dumps([entry['id'], entry.get('revision', entry.get('rev'))], separators=(',', ':'))})

#+class SQLiteOfficeConnection:
#+    """Fictional DB-API fixture running the real generated SELECT statements."""
#+    autocommit = False
#+
#+    def __init__(self, path):
#+        self.connection = sqlite3.connect(path)
#+        self.statements = []
#+
#+    def cursor(self):
#+        return SQLiteOfficeCursor(self, self.connection.cursor())
#+
#+    def rollback(self):
#+        self.connection.rollback()
#+
#+    def close(self):
#+        self.connection.close()
#+
#+
#+class SQLiteOfficeCursor:
#+    def __init__(self, owner, cursor):
#+        self.owner, self.cursor = owner, cursor
#+
#+    def execute(self, sql, parameters=None):
#+        self.owner.statements.append(sql)
#+        if sql == "SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY":
#+            self.cursor.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
#+            self.cursor.execute("BEGIN")
#+        else:
#+            params = {key: value.isoformat(sep=" ") if isinstance(value, datetime) else value
#+                      for key, value in (parameters or {}).items()}
#+            self.cursor.execute(sql, params)
#+        return self
#+
#+    @property
#+    def description(self):
#+        return self.cursor.description
#+
#+    def fetchmany(self, size):
#+        return self.cursor.fetchmany(size)
#+
#+    def close(self):
#+        self.cursor.close()

# FILE {"kind": "app", "name": "docs/37_DAILY_REPORTING_PILOT.txt", "new_lf": true}
#+DAILY REPORTING | DESKTOP PILOT | 27 NOVEMBER 2026
#+
#+Open Reports > Daily Reporting. The default is the previous Zurich business
#+day. Today reads SLD on open, every minute and on Refresh. Historical days
#+read saved source figures together with the latest shared manual entries.
#+The monthly screens continue to use the prepared run.
#+
#+CONFIGURATION
#+
#+Keep the working office connection hook, settings, references and existing
#+output folder. The two new choices in settings.json are daily_reporting.store
#+and daily_reporting.holidays. The store is a separate SQLite file. The
#+relative default ../coupon_lab_reporting/daily.sqlite3 resolves beside the app
#+folder. Point all PCs to the same shared location. The folder needs permission
#+to create the database and its rollback journal. Use a new reporting file.
#+The pricing database has other tables and a different owner. Configure desk
#+holidays as an array of dates in YYYY-MM-DD format. Weekends are skipped.
#+
#+The Windows launcher reads latest.json in the configured monthly output
#+folder and opens that completed run. If it cannot resolve the pointer, open
#+the printed folder and restore the pointer and completed run together or
#+publish a new monthly run using the existing approved preparation. Python
#+selection supports COUPON_LAB_PYTHON on each PC, runtime.python in settings,
#+a local Miniconda installation and PATH. For a named conda environment,
#+set COUPON_LAB_PYTHON to that environment's python.exe. Run on each user's
#+own machine with its approved SLD client and existing Dash environment.
#+
#+RECONCILE THE DAILY QUERIES AT WORK
#+
#+1. Use an office copy and the existing read-only SLD hook. Confirm that
#+   the source timestamps use Zurich local time under that connection.
#+   The read binds local midnight inclusive and next midnight exclusive.
#+   Compare all distinct request IDs created that day with the sum of eFlow,
#+   voiceflow and unresolved requests. Examine the Excel Profile expression.
#+   Manual means voiceflow. A missing profile remains unresolved. Providers
#+   and fee rows must never multiply requests or their amounts.
#+
#+2. Compare trades on product initial-fixing day in ACTIVE or ISSUING state.
#+   Include requests created on a prior date. Check the RFQ/product join for
#+   reprices, the actual product size unit, original currency, displayed
#+   underlying basket, originating platform and executed hedge counterparty.
#+   Inspect a selected winning TRS/DIP/PUT row against the desk's hedge,
#+   including an internally hedged voice trade. A best price alone is
#+   insufficient evidence of execution.
#+
#+3. Use the actual Excel query for the fee calculation. The input for PnL is
#+   an annual decimal margin, notional and lifetime in years. Map the actual
#+   PA/TOTAL fee joins, signs, annualisation and lifetime conventions. A
#+   missing fee must leave the request, trade and original amount visible.
#+   A single record with an independently known PnL is a good scale check.
#+   CHF conversion uses the latest reviewed monthly rate per currency up to
#+   the selected month. Missing amounts and missing rates leave affected totals
#+   unavailable and eFlow threshold eligibility unresolved.
#+
#+4. Put office expressions in settings.json under daily_reporting.source.
#+   Supported overrides, bind names and result aliases are documented at the
#+   top of coupon_lab/daily_source.py, beside the default_queries function.
#+   For instance, flow_profile_expression uses the joined product alias p.
#+   Set profile_confirmed to true only after comparing with the workbook.
#+   Use the reviewed provider mapping before setting provider_confirmed.
#+   Use margin_expression, lifetime_years_expression and margin_scale for
#+   fee-based PnL. A margin already supplied as an annual decimal uses scale
#+   "1"; a percentage-point field uses "0.01" only if verified internally.
#+   Alternatively supply pnl_expression for the reviewed native-currency
#+   Excel result. Verify both the native and the CHF result. Generic pilot
#+   settings cannot supply the missing office fee formula.
#+
#+5. Run a preview until the relevant figures agree. This command reads the
#+   selected date and prints internal aggregate figures and quality indicators.
#+   It does not save a snapshot.
#+
#+   python run_coupon_lab.py daily-capture --date 2026-11-26 --preview
#+
#+   Then click Capture this day from SLD or run the following command.
#+
#+   python run_coupon_lab.py daily-capture --date 2026-11-26
#+
#+   A first capture on a later Zurich date shows Reconstructed. Current SLD
#+   state cannot recreate exactly what the desk knew on Thursday evening.
#+   Save today's snapshot once near close. It records observations through
#+   the displayed read time. Reopening a saved day or repeating the command
#+   preserves its first SLD capture, including the rates used for its trades.
#+
#+MANUAL ENTRIES
#+
#+Choose the reporting day before entering a win, issuance or pre-hedge.
#+A win uses commitment day and may have a provisional or blank size.
#+An issuance uses initial-fixing day and final size. Select the corresponding
#+SLD voice trade if present. An unmatched issuance stays pending until the
#+desk confirms it is missing from SLD. After that confirmation it counts once
#+in addition to the SLD population. Review possible overlaps. If its SLD trade
#+arrives later, link the existing manual row to it. The headline then includes
#+the linked trade once, with its detail in the subscription issuance list.
#+Pre-hedges have their own list. Add or remove entries on any recorded day.
#+After a stale-edit message, refresh, inspect the other user's change, then
#+select the entry again. Original entries and revisions are retained internally.
#+
#+TECHNICAL REFERENCE
#+
#+run_coupon_lab.py handles latest-run startup and daily-capture. daily.py
#+selects the date and live or saved source. daily_source.py queries the two
#+day windows and calculates trade-level amounts. daily_store.py combines
#+manual and source rows and owns SQLite transactions. ui/daily_reporting.py
#+registers the page and its refresh and editor actions.
#+
#+The shared store is an application-owned schema, version 1. No change to
#+an SLD or voice pricing table is required. UTC timestamps are retained
#+alongside Zurich business dates.
#+
#+daily_meta(version) identifies the store schema.
#+daily_snapshots(day PK, source_json, source_hash, saved_at) holds one saved
#+    read per Zurich day. The payload includes request counts and dated trades.
#+daily_entries(id PK, report_day, revision, removed, entry_json) holds each
#+    manual event. The JSON includes its kind, terms and optional SLD trade ID.
#+daily_revisions(entry_id, revision, entry_json) uses the first two columns as
#+    its compound key and retains every manual revision, including removal.
#+daily_retries(token PK, request_hash, result_json, created_at) makes a retry
#+    with the same content return its committed result.
#+
#+For a pilot recovery copy, close all pilot apps and capture commands on both
#+PCs before copying the SQLite file. Keep the backup outside the release
#+directory. Restore to another filename and configure a test app to open the
#+restored file. Confirm the saved day, one manual entry and its revision.
#+Test this procedure and file locking on the actual shared folder.
# FILE {"kind": "companion", "name": "FRIDAY_OFFICE_GUIDE.txt", "new_lf": true}
#+COUPON LAB | FRIDAY OFFICE GUIDE
#+Version daily-reporting-office-20261127-r2
#+Friday 27 November 2026 | Europe/Zurich
#+
#+GOAL AND WORKING ORDER
#+
#+Run the Daily Reporting pilot in a copy of the working office app, reconcile
#+its source and figures, exercise shared additions from two actual PCs, and
#+retain a usable result or a precise failure for the weekend. Use the existing
#+approved internal GPT-5.1 one task at a time. Keep one internal WORKLOG and use
#+WEEKEND_RETURN.txt for the findings you choose to handwrite and bring back.
#+
#+The first version uses SLD for all automatic data. SQLite holds the team's
#+manual entries and saved daily source captures. The older voice pricing
#+database supplies no automatic input to this pilot. Its database and writer
#+remain under their existing owner.
#+
#+The daily report has eFlow and voiceflow figures, trade lists, subscription
#+wins/issuances and separate pre-hedges. It generates no morning-call narrative.
#+Everyone with access to the shared app may run it and edit reporting entries.
#+There is no new app-user allowlist or requirement to record editor identity.
#+
#+Work through these outcomes. Record a missing fact at its affected step, then
#+continue independent tests or an already supported report section.
#+
#+  1. Preserve the working copy and identify each PC's actual interpreter.
#+  2. Save one pasted installer, extract its guide, check and apply the update.
#+  3. Select a separate reporting database and prove initialization/copy safety.
#+  4. Run focused tests and open the latest prepared monthly run.
#+  5. Reconcile a non-saving daily source preview with the desk reference.
#+  6. Test the shared folder with the dedicated fictional two-PC probe.
#+  7. Exercise manual events and refresh in an isolated reporting test store.
#+  8. Verify a consistent backup and isolated restore.
#+  9. Select the live pilot store and save the first accepted source capture.
#+ 10. Check the two mapping rows and Reason exclusions, then record acceptance.

#+also prints internal financial figures, so retain its output internally.

#+then Save day snapshot and reopen; record every untested operation as pending.

#+  echo %ERRORLEVEL%
#+  popd
#+
#+PowerShell equivalent:
#+
#+  Push-Location -LiteralPath '..\Coupon Lab Pilot'
#+  python -B -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_daily*.py' -v
#+  $LASTEXITCODE
#+  Pop-Location
#+
#+The immediate exit code should be 0, with a nonzero discovered test count and
#+no failures/errors. Home installed-copy evidence was 80 Daily tests and an
#+800-test selftest on Python 3.12. Those are synthetic home observations. Record
#+the actual office results. The earlier office baseline reported eight failures
#+and three errors among 722 tests; identify any retained baseline differences
#+separately from new Daily failures. Use the matching full selftest once after
#+focused checks pass:
#+
#+  pushd "..\Coupon Lab Pilot"
#+  python -B run_coupon_lab.py selftest
#+  popd

#+The pushd/popd lines above are CMD syntax; use Push-Location/Pop-Location in
#+PowerShell. Save internal output to a per-PC temporary/evidence folder if you
#+need to inspect a long failure. Include test name, safe stage and actual result
#+in WORKLOG. Zero tests is not a pass. Preserve the office's chosen freshness
#+behavior while diagnosing a baseline mismatch.
#+
#+In settings.json, verify output_folder points to the usual completed monthly
#+outputs. The copied app may need an absolute shared output path if those runs
#+were not copied. Relative paths resolve beside settings.json. latest.json
#+must refer to an existing complete run. Keep its manifest, snapshot and
#+reconciliation files together.

#+Double-click the PILOT Start_Coupon_Lab.bat on each PC. For visible startup
#+diagnostics in CMD from the kit folder:

#+Reports > Daily Reporting. The prior business day is selected by default.
#+An uncaptured past day shows unavailable source figures while manual entries
#+remain available. A missing capture must not become zero activity.

#+occupied local port, run the pilot from its folder with a free local port:

#+5. RECONCILE A DAILY READ BEFORE SAVING IT
#+
#+Start with Thursday 26 November, then Friday 27 November. The diagnostic runner
#+reads the selected source date without opening the manual store or saving an
#+immutable capture:
#+
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" source --date 2026-11-26
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" source --date 2026-11-27
#+
#+EXPECT
#+COMPLETED with request counts, observed trade counts, coverage flags, missing
#+field counts, source-read elapsed time and immutable_capture_written false.
#+COMPLETED means the read ran; it can still report provisional or missing data.
#+An unsupported required source role fails with a stage and next action.
#+
#+For detailed financial reconciliation inside the office, use the app's preview
#+from its own folder. The following is CMD syntax:
#+
#+  pushd "..\Coupon Lab Pilot"
#+  python run_coupon_lab.py daily-capture --date 2026-11-26 --preview
#+  popd
#+
#+Use Push-Location/Pop-Location in PowerShell. The preview does not save the
#+immutable source capture. Keep its real financial output in the office.
#+
#+Complete the desk/Activity Monitor versus preview rows in WEEKEND_RETURN or
#+WORKLOG. Record day, read/cutoff, scope and FX basis. Delta means preview minus
#+the agreed desk/reference value. The original Activity Monitor's created-today,
#+state, channel and required-fee filters may define a different population.
#+Explain that difference instead of requiring unlike cohorts to match blindly.
