#+CHECK THE SOURCE CONTRACT WITH INTERNAL GPT-5.1
#+
#+Requests
#+  Count every distinct SLD request ID on its Zurich creation day, including
#+  each new repricing ID. Provider responses do not add requests. Use local
#+  midnight inclusive and next midnight exclusive. Product/state/fee joins must
#+  preserve requests. eFlow + voiceflow + unknown must conserve the total.
#+  Verify the actual Excel Manual-profile expression at work. Missing profile
#+  remains unknown. The pilot's p.USER_PROFILE_NAME candidate is provisional
#+  until checked; profile_confirmed is an evidence flag, not a shortcut.
#+
#+Trades
#+  Use eligible product INITIAL_FIXING on the selected day, including products
#+  created earlier. Verify ACTIVE/ISSUING against the actual desk execution
#+  rule. Confirm request.ISSUE_ID -> product.ID and the selected RFQ/product
#+  revision/cardinality. Distinguish eligible rows from unresolved/orphan links.
#+  Incomplete trade coverage withholds headline totals while keeping matched
#+  and unresolved rows visible. Request counts can still be usable separately.
#+
#+Terms and provider
#+  Verify SLD amount units, original currency, product type, underlyings and
#+  platform. The earlier voice pricing size_mln field does not establish SLD
#+  AMOUNT units. Inspect an actual executing hedge, including an internal one.
#+  Resolve the singular/plural hedge-table spelling. Multiple quotes or the
#+  best-price view do not establish the executed provider. Set provider_confirmed
#+  only after checking the actual winner/part/revision rule.
#+
#+FX
#+  Use the reviewed existing monthly CHF_per_unit reference. The pilot chooses
#+  each currency's latest available reviewed month on or before the selected
#+  reporting month and retains the used rate/month. CHF identity is one.
#+  Check the quote direction, native units, reviewed flag/provenance and missing
#+  cells. Missing foreign FX retains the trade/original amount and makes its
#+  CHF contribution unavailable. A partial CHF sum must not look complete.
#+  Test CHF 499,999, 500,000 and 500,001: only the last enters the strict large-
#+  eFlow detail list. Test foreign conversion before that threshold decision.
#+
#+PnL
#+  First issuance PnL is annual decimal margin x native notional x years.
#+  Map the actual Excel PA/TOTAL fees, signs, joins and lifetime convention.
#+  The default pilot has no invented margin/lifetime formula. NULL inputs keep
#+  affected PnL unavailable while preserving the request, trade and original size.
#+  An office-confirmed PNL_NATIVE alias can supply the native result directly.
#+
#+FICTIONAL SCALE CHECK
#+  Suppose raw annual margin is 1.2 percentage points, native USD notional is
#+  1,000,000 and lifetime is 1.5 years. margin_scale 0.01 gives decimal 0.012.
#+  Native PnL = 1.2 x 0.01 x 1,000,000 x 1.5 = USD 18,000.
#+  With an invented CHF-per-USD factor 0.90, notional = CHF 900,000 and
#+  PnL = CHF 16,200. This product exceeds CHF 500,000.
#+  If raw annual margin is already 0.012, its scale is 1 instead. If the verified
#+  lifetime input is 18 months under a simple months/12 convention, the SELECT
#+  returns 1.5 as LIFETIME_YEARS. Use the desk's actual convention once confirmed.
#+  This invented example checks representation and arithmetic. It supplies no
#+  real FX rate, booked profit or office product-specific fee definition.
#+
#+PROMPT FOR INTERNAL GPT-5.1
#+Inspect coupon_lab/daily_source.py and our Activity Monitor SQL/Power Query
#+internally. Verify the request and initial-fixing populations, exact Manual
#+profile, winning hedge, units and PA/TOTAL financial joins. Reuse the approved
#+office connection and existing runtime. Put reviewed expressions under
#+daily_reporting.source in settings.json. Preserve missing values and one RFQ
#+per trade. Use profile_confirmed/provider_confirmed only after verification.
#+The module documents canonical aliases and :day_start/:day_end binds. If needed,
#+save the reviewed full SELECT in an office-local request_sql_file/trade_sql_file
#+relative to settings.json. Return PNL_NATIVE or verified MARGIN_PA,
#+LIFETIME_YEARS and explicit margin_scale. Run preview, compare the desk table,
#+explain one mismatch and rerun the affected case. Keep our private SQL internal.
#+
#+IF IT FAILS
#+Keep the stage, source role/field and suggested action. A required read failure
#+leaves the last-good source unchanged. Optional provider/FX/PnL gaps remain
#+explicit. Keep independently checked figures progressing. Do not save a real
#+day just to hide an unresolved required population error.

#+6. RUN THE DEDICATED TWO-PC SHARED-STORE PROBE
#+
#+Both PCs use the same pilot settings/store path. The diagnostic derives a
#+separate sibling TEST database named <store stem>.office-share-test.sqlite3.
#+For the default live filename this is daily.office-share-test.sqlite3. If you
#+selected daily_friday_test.sqlite3, its probe is
#+daily_friday_test.office-share-test.sqlite3. It writes no production entries.
#+
#+Run from each PC's kit folder with its own approved Python environment:
#+
#+PC A, initialize once:
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-init
#+
#+PC B, inspect the same probe:
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-check
#+
#+EXPECT
#+The same random probe_id and current_revision 1 on both PCs. Re-running
#+share-init retains the existing marked test probe. If a prior test already
#+advanced its revision, record the actual value N and use N consistently below.
#+
#+PC A, accept one update and retain its explicit retry token:
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-update --expected-revision 1 --token friday-A-update-1
#+
#+Repeat that SAME command on A:
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-update --expected-revision 1 --token friday-A-update-1
#+
#+EXPECT
#+Both return the accepted operation revision 2. The replay creates no additional
#+revision/entry. Use a new token for a genuinely new intentional operation. If
#+you restarted an existing probe at N, replace expected revision 1 with N and
#+use a new distinct token tied to that test attempt.
#+
#+PC B, deliberately attempt the stale revision with a DIFFERENT token:
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-update --expected-revision 1 --token friday-B-stale-1
#+
#+EXPECT
#+CONFLICT and exit 3. Check the immediate code in CMD with echo %ERRORLEVEL%,
#+or in PowerShell with $LASTEXITCODE. A stale conflict is the intended result.
#+Then B runs:
#+
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "..\Coupon Lab Pilot" share-check
#+
#+EXPECT
#+The same probe_id, current_revision 2 and retained revision history. Restart
#+the clients/terminals and repeat share-check on both PCs. Verify the same
#+committed state. To test changed-content reuse, reuse A's token with expected
#+revision 2; expect retry_conflict/exit 1 and no third revision. Then share-check
#+must still show revision 2. Record actual lock/wait/errors and timing.
#+
#+IF IT FAILS
#+Different probe IDs mean different physical files or path selection. Confirm
#+the same share/path internally; mapped drive names can differ between PCs.
#+A path-reference hash is local evidence, not cross-PC file identity. Busy/lock
#+errors need the actual filesystem/permissions and supported locking assessment.
#+Do not repeatedly create replacement databases to disguise a failed shared
#+write test. Two processes on one PC remain a useful narrower test; record
#+two-PC acceptance as pending if only one PC is available.

#+1. THE COPY AND THE INSTALL
#+
#+Date/time and kit version =
#+Working copy or source revision, using an internal alias if appropriate =

#+Double-click opened latest prepared run without monthly preparation =

# FILE {"kind": "companion", "name": "RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt", "new_lf": true}
#+"""Run local Coupon Lab office checks using Python's standard library.
#+
#+Run this TXT directly. The interpreter must be the one used by the app.
#+
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "APP_FOLDER" environment
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "APP_FOLDER" source --date 2026-11-27
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "APP_FOLDER" store-init
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "APP_FOLDER" store-check
#+  python RUN_OFFICE_CHECKS.py.txt --app "APP_FOLDER" store-copy --source "OLD.sqlite3" --destination "NEW.sqlite3"
#+
#+Global --settings SETTINGS_FILE selects another settings JSON (relative to app).
#+Relative input/store paths follow that settings file's folder, as in the app.
#+Global --output NEW_REPORT.json additionally saves the printed JSON. The output
#+file must be new. Neither option is required. Outputs contain fixed metadata,
#+aggregate counts, selected dates/months and random synthetic probe identifiers.
#+They omit records, monetary totals, source SQL, paths and driver exceptions.
#+
#+source calls the installed daily_source.read_day, using the existing read-only
#+office hook. It never opens the manual store or saves an immutable capture.
#+store-init initializes the selected daily_reporting.store with the installed
#+DailyStore. store-check requires an existing current-format reporting store.
#+Foreign/pricing/prototype schemas are rejected before any store writes.
#+store-copy only accepts the current reporting schema, opens its source read-only,
#+uses SQLite backup into a new destination, and checks integrity/counts. It
#+performs no migration and never overwrites an existing destination.
#+
#+Optional two-PC checks use ONLY a separate sibling of the reporting store named
#+<store stem>.office-share-test.sqlite3. No real reporting entries are written.
#+  PC A: ... share-init
#+  PC B: ... share-check
#+  PC A: ... share-update --expected-revision 1
#+  PC B: ... share-update --expected-revision 1     (CONFLICT, exit code 3)
#+  PC B: ... share-check                         (same probe ID, revision 2)
#+--token TEXT on share-update permits retry of the same expected-revision action.
#+Run share-init first on A, then the B checks. Re-running share-init retains the
#+existing marked probe. A file lacking the dedicated probe marker is rejected.
#+Local process checks do not establish network filesystem locking/support.
#+
#+Exit codes: 0 completed, 1 check/setup failure, 2 invalid arguments, 3 stale
#+revision conflict. A completed source read may report unresolved data.
#+"""
